# RU_A â€” roop-ultimate backend audit (slice A)

Reference repo: roop-ultimate @ 71ebb65 (read-only clone). Scope: `app/roop/` (289), `app/core/`, `app/clip/`, `app/ui/`, `app/sidecar_keep/`, `app/tools/`, `app/docs/` and every top-level `app/*` file (selected with the assignment regex, incl. non-code top-level files such as README/Dockerfile/LICENSE/yaml/`--help`).
Method: AST index of every file (docstring, top-level symbols with line numbers, stub-keyword counts) + 9 parallel per-group readers who read headers, constants, route decorators, argparse blocks and fallback paths; very large files (ProcessMgr.py 450 KB, procmgr_tracking.py 230 KB, api.py 379 KB, face_util.py 181 KB, runtime_optimizer.py 163 KB, FaceSwapInsightFace.py 136 KB, core.py 122 KB, optimized_processor.py 119 KB, procmgr_masking.py 118 KB, settings.py 115 KB) were outlined and read by targeted ranges, **not line-by-line**. Evidence paths are normalised to repo-relative (`app/...:line`); a few bare short names in prose are relative to `app/roop/` (stated per group in the origin tag). Unverified claims are tagged `[INFERENCE]`.

## Executive summary (what this reference actually is)
- **Pure 2D swap pipeline.** SCRFD/RetinaFace detect -> 5-pt (or 106-pt) similarity alignment -> inswapper-class 128 px tile (pixel-boost tiles up to `subsample_size`) -> mask/feather/colour transfer -> optional restorer -> paste. No volumetric/.fsz 3D code exists; "3D" = `use_3d_recon` (PnP + 2D flip/shear, `fit_source`/`render_from_coefficients` are no-op stubs), frontalization, pose-binned source bank; `.fsz` is a ZIP of `N.png` + `metadata.json` v2 (RUA_GrpA, frag A).
- **Strength is engineering around the 2D path**: Kalman+Hungarian identity tracks with stitching/coasting, One-Euro and zero-phase smoothing, histogram scene-cut detectors, TensorRT/ORT ladder with precision policy + VRAM governor, segmented resumable video writer with sha256 manifests, persistent queue, WebSocket telemetry, terminal dashboard, React workstation API (66 inline routes + 29 routers).
- **Many advanced modules are opt-in, unwired, or contract-only** (see Gaps): `fused_postprocess`, `l2_persistence`, `vectorized_pipeline`, `inference_optimizer`, `unified_runtime_scheduler`, `trt_engine`, `worker_pool`, `video_io_hw` zero-copy classes, `JournalManager`, `final_quality_gate`, `stage17_audit`; `UltraMax compile` route is simulated; `tile_diffusion` is not diffusion.
- Numerous features were *measured worse* and are shipped OFF (bystander veto, flow-align, hi-res redetect, nonfrontal mask routing, mask fusion, occluder neutral on synthetic occluders).

## api.py infrastructure [E1a]
- CORS: `allow_origin_regex=^https?://(localhost|127.0.0.1|[::1]|*.localhost)(:port)?$`, credentials True, methods/headers `*`, exposes Content-Range/Accept-Ranges/Content-Length/Content-Type (app/api.py:111-120).
- Auth: outermost `AccessControlMiddleware` (app/api.py:122): foreign Origin -> 403 "Origin not allowed"; share mode needs token (Bearer / `?token=` / cookie `roop_api_token`) else 401; WS refused with close 1008; OPTIONS bypasses token (app/api_access.py:190-215). Non-share = no token at all (app/api_access.py:108-109). Origin check passes when no Origin header (app/api_access.py:81-82).
- Upload errors -> 400 `{detail}` (app/api.py:125-128). Upload roots registered: temp/api_uploads, temp, .pinokio-temp (app/api.py:304-312).
- WebSocket: no @app.websocket in api.py; `/ws/telemetry` lives in app/routes_telemetry.py:289 (outside group). Lifespan (app/api.py:7243-7264) binds loop + starts `hub.start_sampler(_telemetry_snapshot)` (side-effect-free snapshot, app/api.py:7091-7240), assigned via `app.router.lifespan_context` (not constructor/on_event). No SSE anywhere; StreamingResponse only used for in-memory multipart-ish preview_grid/preview_seq (app/api.py:2995,3178).
- Static UI: serves react-ui/dist; `/assets` mount + 404 handler SPA fallback; 503 HTML "React UI Build Missing" if dist/index.html absent (app/api.py:7270-7512).
- Concurrency guards: `_preview_request_lock` (app/api.py:131); 409 on preview while render active (app/api.py:197-203); 503 busy retry_after_ms=1000 during pre-pass stage (app/api.py:206-222); 503 "initializing" retry 500ms until CFG published (app/api.py:225-232); stale-selection guard via `_selection_versions` (app/api.py:140-146); /api/swap 409 if processing or benchmark running (app/api.py:5611-5622), 400 if no target/source.
- Angle-bank gates (env-overridable, scipy cosine distance): ROOP_ANGLE_MANUAL_MAX 0.90, ACCEPT 0.60, SEED_MAX 0.85, MIN_QUALITY 0.35, MIN_PX 64, LONE_ACCEPT 0.55, REVIEW 0.70 (app/api.py:321-373). BLUR_FRAC/WARMUP owned by face_quality.
- Pose bins (axis 1): coverage-only pose from 5 kps, yaw=log(|nose-lex|/|rex-nose|), pitch=log(|ny-eye_y|/|mouth_y-ny|); YAW_EDGES (-1.20,-0.75,-0.43,-0.15,0.15,0.43,0.75,1.20) (9 bins), PITCH_EDGES (-0.75,-0.43,0.37,0.75) (5 bins) (app/api.py:1993-2042). Heuristic keypoint ratio, not a head-pose solver. UI label "Front" ~Â±45Â° yaw (app/api.py:1991).
- Pre-pass/tracking: pose-transition re-scan of coarse sweep gaps (app/api.py:2045-2065); pause controller (`_procmgr_runtime.pause_controller`) for /api/pause,/resume (app/api.py:6338-6366).

## Appendix: run.py CLI arguments (run.py:164-228) and env [E1a]
`--cuda_device_id` int 0; `--execution-provider` ; `--execution-batch-size` (batch_size_arg, beats perf_batch_max); `--source/--source-path`; `--resume`; `--project`; `--render`; `--output`; `--export-fcpxml`; `--export-edl`; `--scene-detect` (all except --project need --project else parser.error); `--benchmark`; `--benchmark-faces {1,2,all}` default 1; `--benchmark-mode {quick,full,regression}` default quick (quick ~30s, full ~90s, regression = real 300-frame 1080p render with fps/VRAM/latency/SSIM/PSNR); `--benchmark-frames` 300; `--benchmark-clip`; `--benchmark-source`; `--benchmark-threads`; `--benchmark-update-baseline`; `--benchmark-apply`; `--ui {react,gradio,legacy}`; `--react`; `--diagnose-runtime`. api_access also reads argv `--server_share` (not in argparse: run.py parser would reject unknown arg [INFERENCE: argparse.parse_args errors on --server_share unless defined elsewhere]).
Env set/read: PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF, OPENCV_LOG_LEVEL=ERROR, AV_LOG_LEVEL=error, ROOP_REACT_CLIENT, ROOP_HEADLESS_PROJECT, ROOP_API_PORT (8001), ROOP_ANGLE_* (7 names), ROOP_PREVIEW_PNG, and ENV_SETTINGS exports (ROOP_TRT_POOL, TRT_BUILDER_OPT_LEVEL, TRT_AUX_STREAMS, TRT_CUDA_GRAPH, CV_THREADS, ORT_INTRA/INTER_THREADS, FFMPEG_THREADS, DETMASK_POOL, DETECTOR_POOL, EXPR_POOL, ENCODER_PRESET, STAB_CHUNK_MB, STAB_STREAMING, CUDA_ARENA_STRATEGY, CUDNN_CONV_ALGO, CUDA_MEM_LIMIT, PROFILE, BATCH_SWAP(+_XFRAME), NVDEC, BATCH_SWAP_MAX, NVENC_PRESET, GPU_AFFINE, PINNED_BUFFERS, TEMPORAL_STEP, PREPASS_DETECT_INTERVAL, PREPASS_FILTER, ENHANCE_MIN_FACE_PX, AUTO_BYPASS_RESTORER_NATIVE_SWAP, TEMPORAL_SMART_SKIP, SPECULAR_HIGHLIGHT_RECOVERY, AMBIENT_SHADOW_MATCH, SUBDERMAL_WARMTH_MATCH, HF_FLOW_{BACKEND,VRAM,WEIGHTS,CYCLE_SIGMA,BILATERAL_SIGMA}, FACE_DEMARCATE, TRACK_STITCH, VERIFY_SWAP, UPRIGHT_REMEASURE, ADAFACE, HDR, HDR_TRANSFER, HDR_PRIMARIES, HDR_CODEC, IDENTITY_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD, PRIORITY) (app/settings.py:138-210); explicit env wins except mib_to_bytes (app/settings.py:268-271).
Startup flow: DLL dirs registered -> (main only) run_preflight_checks (BOOT, DEPENDENCY, DLL_RUNTIME, ORT, GPU, PROVIDER_ADMISSION phases, app/run.py:86-105) -> UTF-8 stdout -> _apply_perf_env (RuntimeOptimizer.startup_profile + settings.apply_env) -> CPU topology log -> win asyncio compat -> argparse -> diagnose-runtime exit -> batch size -> provider override into globals.execution_providers -> main: --resume | --benchmark(regression/other) | headless project render via core.run() | else keep_awake.boost_process_priority, start run_api daemon thread, core.run(), then (React) API_READY/UI_READY phases + join loop (app/run.py:325-435).

## Appendix: Benchmark metrics [D]
- Standard window 150 frames (5 s @30 fps), master clip 300 frames 1920x1080@30; autotune screen 100f / confirm 600f; acceptance 600f; ranking 120f (app/roop/benchmark/asset_manager.py:41-46; app/roop/benchmark/autotune.py:48-49; app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:98-103). Metrics stored: avg_fps, p1_low_fps, peak_vram_mb, peak_cpu_pct (app/roop/benchmark/storage.py:144). Dual-target required metrics: baseline_fps, final_fps, improvement_pct, peak/avg VRAM, CPU/GPU util, decode/inference/enhancement/encode throughput, latency_ms, stability, output_quality (app/roop/hardware_validation.py:19-26). Bench profiles: quick 1.0 s measure / pools (1,); full 1.5 s x2 reps / pools (1,2,3,4,6) / est 450 s; providers est 120 s; stress 12 s x2 / default faces 2.0 / est 900 s (app/roop/bench.py:79-155). Thread/pool gain bars THREAD_GAIN 5%, POOL_GAIN 4%, stability spread 20% (app/roop/bench.py:168-181). Regression thresholds: face SSIM mean >=.97, per-frame min >=.90, face PSNR >=35, frame PSNR >=40, coverage drop <=.02, fps warn 15% advisory (app/roop/benchmark/regression.py:75-92).

## Axis findings (slice)
Bullets are tagged `[group]` (see Group map). Evidence is `file:line`.

### (1) Detection / landmarks / angle estimation + failure thresholds
- [F] Config detection defaults: `detector_engine: retinaface_r50`, `face_detector_threshold 0.5`, `face_detector_nms 0.3`, `face_detector_size '512'`, `max_face_distance 0.75`, `refine_landmarks true`, `rescue_small_faces true`, `autorotate_faces true`, `detector_scale_pyramid auto`, `upright_remeasure auto` (`app/default_config.yaml:20,31-34,57,101-102,158`). Schema defaults differ: `face_detector_size 640` (`settings.schema.json`, key `face_detector_size`).
- [F] Pose gates are in backend constants only named in `--help` (face_util: `FACE_ROLL_LOWER/UPPER`, `POSE_ALIGN_ONSET_DEG`, `POSE_ALIGN_FULL_DEG`, `YAW_ALIGN_RATIO`; no values dumped) â€” [INFERENCE] values not recoverable from this group.
- [F] Occlusion landmark-symmetry repair: refused past `|yaw| > 55Â°` because `solve_pose_5pt` is "measured 15â€“20Â° off per person"; mirror map accepted only as an involution `m[m[i]]==i`, scale-free tolerance; works for any landmark count (`docs/PHASE2_OCCLUSION.md` Â§2).
- [F] UI face selection modes: "First found", "All input faces", "All female", "All male", "All faces", "Selected face" (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:30`). No-face actions: "Use untouched original frame", "Retry rotated", "Skip Frame", "Skip Frame if no similar face", "Use last swapped" (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:29`); default `Use untouched original frame` (`app/default_config.yaml:71`).
- [F] UI "Max Face Similarity Threshold" slider 0.01â€“1.0, "0.0 = identical 1.0 = no similarity" (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:251`); Face Mgr cut uses `extract_face_images(filename,(True,frame_num),0.5)` threshold 0.5 (`facemgr_tab.py:~147`).
- [F] Recognition calibration (equal-error cosine-distance thresholds from 16-clip set): default w600k 0.700, adaface 0.698, glintr100 0.704, antelopev2 0.704, mobilefacenet 0.631, facerecognizersf 0.557 (`app/tools/compare_recognition_swap.py:52-53`). Min face 48 px; dup IoU 0.35; link IoU 0.5; cut diff 25.0 on 64x36 thumbs (`app/tools/calibrate_recognition.py:42-46`). Only w600k and AdaFace are wired into the real pipeline's matching; other four are offline emulation (`compare_recognition_swap.py` docstring).
- [F] Selection-invariance (audit): CPU vs CUDA vs TRT per-frame swap decision identical 119/119 for both persons on `double/d1.mp4`; selection sensitivity (two faces) NOT TESTED; `double/d2,d6` selected-single-person yields 0/200 composited â€” "known open lead single-person fallback on multi-face content" (`RUNTIME_PROVIDER_AUDIT.md` Â§4).
- [E1a] Detection defaults in settings: detector_engine `retinaface_r50`, face_detector_size 640 (320 if default_det_size False), threshold 0.50, nms 0.3, detector_scale_pyramid auto, rescue_small_faces True, temporal_detection True, autorotate_faces True, max_face_distance 0.75, face_detection_mode "Selected face" (app/settings.py:1098-1106,1352-1356). Pose = keypoint-ratio bins only (app/api.py:1993-2042); no pitch/yaw failure threshold in group beyond identity_lock_margin 0.08/hold 3 (app/settings.py:1349-1350) and frontalization_threshold 15.0 deg (app/settings.py:1162, off by default `use_frontalization` False).
- [D] Detector static input pinned 640x640 for scrfd/retinaface family under TRT (app/roop/trt_shape_profile.py:113-124); detector_resolution autotune 512/640/768/960 (app/roop/runtime_optimizer.py:1590-1605). Detect pre-pass (optimized_prepass.PrepassConfig): interval 5, dim 640, min score 0.0, max faces 32, 5-pt fallback from fixed box ratios (0.32/0.38, .68/.38, .5/.55, .37/.72, .63/.72) (app/roop/optimized_prepass.py:98-160,206-226). RetinaFaceDecoder conf .5 NMS .4 top_k 64 (app/roop/vectorized_pipeline.py:535-545; unwired).
- [D] No yaw/pitch estimation code lives in group D. Benchmark scenarios assume yaw 30-50 deg (45deg_face), 70-80 deg (extreme profile) and a ~56x56 px small-face (app/roop/benchmark/scenario_assets.py:83-203); `quality_metrics` profile failure = zero detections on `is_profile` frames (app/roop/benchmark/quality_metrics.py:164-168) and uses supplied ground-truth yaw, not an estimator.
- [D] Identity-mismatch threshold in bench quality: cosine < 0.20 counted as incorrect assignment (app/roop/benchmark/quality_metrics.py:208); regression changed-face MAD 4.0/255 (5.6x the measured 0.714 noise floor) (app/roop/benchmark/regression.py:70-73).
- [B] All swap models use 5-pt kps alignment; no 3D mesh. Template per model: arcface (inswapper/reswapper/hyperswap/alpha), arcface_112_v1 (ghost, simswap), ffhq_512 (blendswap, uniface, cscs, instyleswapper A/B/C), mtcnn_512 (hififace) â€” app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:113-533.
- [B] Yaw from 5-pt: `face_util.solve_pose_jaw_5pt` (outside group) used by RealSwap: hififace eye-band fades for |yaw|>=35 deg (ROOP_REALSWAP_LATERAL_FADE_DEG) and is fully off at 65 deg (_LATERAL_SKIP_DEG) â€” app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:1773-1774,2077-2091.
- [B] Outcome guard (swap_moved_the_face): per-model verify_tol; only hififace sets 0.65 (default global SWAP_MOVED_TOL=1.0 in face_util); calibrated on ONE synthetic clip; hyperswap/inswapper clean-vs-wrecked bands overlap (0.79 vs 0.79 / 0.60) so not tightened â€” app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:206-233,407,567.
- [B] Legacy frame swapper: roll normalisation when |theta|>45 deg (build_canonical_rotation_matrix threshold_deg=45.0); profile-aware weighted Umeyama when |yaw|>35 â€” app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:1881-1990 (comments at ~1900-1975). Fallback stubs of these helpers return identity/None (app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:263-292).
- [B] Adaptive enhancer pose score = 1 - max(|yaw|,|pitch|)/65; gates: pose<0.18, conf<0.28, occlusion>0.72, temporal<0.28 => no enhancer; quality cut FAST 0.63 / BALANCED 0.68 / REALISTIC 0.72 / MAX 0.76 (hysteresis +-0.06); 5-pt fallback yaw = |nose-eyemid|/IOD*45 â€” app/roop/adaptive_enhancer.py:87-104,172-200.
- [B] Restore Ultra optimizer damps for |yaw|>25: eye factor max(0.55,1-(|yaw|-25)/70), edge factor max(0.70,1-(|yaw|-25)/90); crispness multiplier clamp [0.35,1.10] â€” app/roop/restore_ultra_optimizer.py:291-304.
- [B] MAR (mouth open): 68-pt = (|p61-p67|+|p62-p66|+|p63-p65|)/(2|p60-p64|), threshold default 0.18 (clamped 0.05-0.50); 106-pt path is a different estimator (r=0.49 vs 68-pt); 5-pt returns constant 0.10 â€” app/roop/enhancers/mouth_dynamics.py:57-131,137-141.
- [B] EAR blink threshold: code const EAR_BLINK_THRESHOLD=0.18, module docstring says 0.21 (contradiction) â€” app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:16,320.
- [B] XSeg3 mask-cache recompute triggers: |d yaw/pitch/roll|>2.5 deg, landmark displacement >1.8 px, d confidence < -0.15 â€” app/roop/xseg3_optimizer.py:16-24 (docstring).
- [B] Enhance gate: skip restorer when face px < ROOP_ENHANCE_MIN_FACE_PX (default 0 = disabled), resume at 1.15x threshold â€” app/roop/enhance_gate.py:33,38-41,90.
- [E2] Not in this group. Only related: live tracker keeps face points from `landmark_2d_106`â†’`landmark_3d_68`â†’`kps` (first with â‰¥3 finite 2D points), live_mode.py:~232-243. No yaw/pose limits anywhere in E2 files.
- [E2] Live LK-flow reject gates: <max(3, 55%) surviving points â†’ keyframe re-detect; median LK error >35.0 â†’ reject; RANSAC `estimateAffinePartial2D` reproj 3.0 px; winSize (15,15), maxLevel 2, 10 iters/eps 0.03, live_mode.py:~268-286.
- [E2] Watchdog: all-zero/NaN/Inf tensor = anomaly; mask area drop to 0 or >70% jump (prev â‰¥100 px) within same scene cut and consecutive frame, app/roop/watchdog.py:380-431; zero-swap alert after 5 consecutive frames, app/roop/watchdog.py:346-357.
- [A] Detector engines (roop.globals.detector_engine, default 'scrfd' globals.py:292): scrfd (insightface buffalo_l det_10g), yoloface, retinaface (10g), retinaface_r50, retinaface_r50_gpu, yunet (`_HYBRID_ENGINES` app/roop/face_util.py:79). Hybrid engines pop buffalo_l's own detection session and reuse its aux models (app/roop/face_util.py:291-293).
- [A] Defaults: face_detector_size '640' (globals.py:58), face_detector_threshold 0.50 (globals.py:59; _build_face_analyser fallback 0.60 at app/roop/face_util.py:267), face_detector_nms 0.40 (globals.py:60). `_desired_det_size` clamps 320..1280 (app/roop/face_util.py:70). scrfd/retinaface/retinaface_r50 are FORCED to 640 regardless of setting (app/roop/face_util.py:1076-1077,1089-1090). yoloface export is fixed 640 (app/roop/yoloface.py:62-96); yunet scales longest side to det_size, score .6 / nms .3 at creation, then per-call thresh (app/roop/yunet.py:29,100-117); R50 priors min_sizes ((16,32),(64,128),(256,512)), steps (8,16,32), variance (.1,.2) (app/roop/retinaface.py:47-49).
- [A] Landmark counts: 5-pt kps (all engines), landmark_2d_106 (always in allowed modules, app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:1333), landmark_3d_68 (insightface 3D-68 coords, loaded when use_3d_recon/use_source_bank/use_frontalization/refine_landmarks/autorotate, app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:1329-1335; 'lazy' for autorotate-only, app/roop/face_util.py:289-290). No 203/478-pt landmarks anywhere in group A (only mentions: none found in scanned symbols).
- [A] Pose: primary estimator is `solve_pose_5pt` weak-perspective pinv solve against `_REF3D_68`-derived 5-pt head (app/roop/face_util.py:2818-2970); '<0.05 deg on synthetic grid yaw 0-90, pitch +/-40, roll +/-30' (docstring 2948-2950). `solve_pose_jaw_5pt` adds a 6th DOF for mouth opening, JAW_SOLVE_MIN/MAX -0.35/0.90 (app/roop/face_util.py:2919-2920). `pose_quality.estimate_head_pose` = perspective EPnP + solvePnPRefineLM against same ref head, rotated to line of sight (app/roop/pose_quality.py:131-168). face_analyser.estimate_head_pose_pnp uses a different canonical 3D-5 template (app/roop/face_analyser.py:63-69,138). Sign convention: +yaw = face toward viewer's right, +pitch = tilted DOWN (app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:9-22).
- [A] Angle bins (app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:91-136): 9-bin lattice on up-positive pitch: FRONTAL |yaw|<=10 & |p|<=10; QUARTER L/R yaw 10-25; HALF_PROFILE L/R yaw 25-45 (|p|<=15); PROFILE L/R |yaw|>45 (any pitch); PITCH_UP/DOWN |yaw|<=20 & |p|>15. Gaps exist; MAX_FILL_DEG=10 nearest-fill (app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:54). Per-person neutral pitch (median of |yaw|<=25) subtracted (140-160).
- [A] FaceSet V2 yaw ladder POSE_BINS (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:37-40,226-240): frontal <=10, mild <=25, medium <=45, strong <=70, profile >70. 3x3 pose matrix edges yaw +/-25, pitch +/-15 (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:89-93). Pose-Radar buckets (app/roop/identity_lab.py:21-31): profile |yaw|>=40, angle |pitch|>=20.
- [A] Profile thresholds elsewhere: alignment switches at |yaw|>=45 or |pitch|>=30 (app/roop/face_analyser.py:660-667); source_portfolio PROFILE_YAW_DEG 35 (app/roop/source_portfolio.py:46); adaptive_detector profile_yaw_threshold 45 (app/roop/adaptive_detector.py:51); geometric_alignment semi 20/full 45 (app/roop/geometric_alignment.py:42-43); nonfrontal off-axis max 50 deg, yaw-ratio min .55, asym max .25, pitch band .32-.70 (app/roop/nonfrontal.py:49-54); source_gallery label heuristic ratio <.65 / >1.55 (app/source_gallery.py:63-66); face_3d_recon flip needs |yaw|>=20 both sides, opposite sign, |delta|>=45 (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:190-191).
- [A] Roll/upside-down: FACE_ROLL_LOWER 54.5, FACE_ROLL_UPPER 135 (app/roop/face_util.py:2498-2499); orientation.py uses 45/135 with hysteresis 36/54 and 126/144 (app/roop/orientation.py:45-46,171-210); track latch TRUST_DEG 40, MAX_COAST 45 (app/roop/orientation.py:53,58); lm68 arm at 20 deg, probe every 12, hold 90, disagree 45 (app/roop/face_util.py:1427-1433). Detector hallucinates upright face at roll 140-210 (app/roop/face_util.py:2515-2519).
- [A] Failure/rescue ladder when first pass empty (app/roop/face_util.py:1918-1957): upscale x2 (only if rescue_small_faces, default False globals.py:287) -> downscaled 320 with thresh max(0.30, thr-0.15) -> padded 25% -> rotated -> CLAHE; plus partial-miss rotated passes for expected_count (1969-1992). A second-detector rescue was measured and REJECTED (app/roop/face_util.py:1959-1965).
- [A] Pyramid: auto-trigger when face h>=500px or >=75% of frame, scales [0.5,0.75,1.0], 64px context pad, DIoU-NMS (app/roop/face_detector.py:53-57,394-437,676-681); explicit via --detector-scale-pyramid.
- [A] Dedup/touching faces: NMS suppresses only if IoU>thr AND centre sep <=0.25 face-widths (app/roop/nms.py:50,97-102); phantom 'junction' box removal thresholds in app/roop/face_contact.py:92-148; sanity validator drops faces with bad bbox/kps (face_sanity.py header).
- [A] Landmark sanity validators: adaptive_detector interocular>=3px etc. (app/roop/adaptive_detector.py:127-135); capture_seed.landmarks_plausible mouth below eyes by 5% box, nose between (app/roop/capture_seed.py:57-86).
- [A] Quality gates for angle capture: MIN_IOD_PX 45 frontal-equivalent, luma 40..215, clipped <=0.25, EAR>=0.20, blur relative 0.5*median after 8 samples (app/roop/pose_quality.py:79-88; app/roop/face_quality.py:143-165; app/roop/pose_quality.py:290-296).
- [C1] Track appearance gate EMB_MAX default 0.7 (`ROOP_TRACK_EMB_MAX`), Re-ID vs retired track 0.5 (`ROOP_TRACK_REID_MAX`), IOU_MIN 0.2, STALE 15 frames: app/roop/procmgr_runtime.py:261,305; app/roop/procmgr_tracking.py:428.
- [C1] Identity veto 0.85 (`ROOP_TRACK_VETO`), single-person absolute veto OFF (0), margin 0.15, assign max 0.6, assign margin 0.15, floor 0.45 (AdaFace floor 0.45), selected hold 0.95, unbound-same 0.65, min obs 3, overlap frac 0.15: app/roop/procmgr_runtime.py:219,239,265,458,516,538,555,569,585,481,489.
- [C1] Locked-track detector-confidence floor LOCK_MIN_DET=0.85: app/roop/procmgr_runtime.py:1037. Verify-swap outcome gate on by default, VERIFY_AWAY_YAW 88.0, MOVED_TOL 0.5, SHAPE_TOL 0.3: app/roop/procmgr_runtime.py:973-1020.
- [C1] Hi-res miss redetect OFF by default (HIRES_DET_SIZE 960), ROI-crop redetect OFF; both documented as measured regressions: app/roop/procmgr_tracking.py:321,363-364. Prepass rescue every 8 frames: tracking:515.
- [C1] Rotation retry: retry_rotated rotates frame 90 CW/CCW and re-runs swap_faces; accepted only if `rotation_improves_upright` unless track-resolved roll: app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:3605-3660, 5693-5710. Rotated paste-back uses `_unrotate_face_to_parent` into full-frame space.
- [C1] Partial-miss ROI rescue default ON (`ROOP_NO_PARTIAL_MISS_RESCUE`): app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:231; per-worker last_found_bboxes app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:3913. ROI pad = 0.6*max(w,h) (verify), app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5310.
- [C1] Frontalization (Option 2) only if `use_frontalization`; default threshold 25.0 deg pitch/yaw; via roop.face_frontalize.frontalize_crop: app/roop/ProcessOptions.py:23, app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:6193-6215. Pose via estimate_pose on 68-lm crop: app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5861.
- [C1] Mask recovery: undersized-mask floor from 5 kps ellipse, trigger 0.5 of floor area, `ROOP_MASK_RECOVER` default on: app/roop/procmgr_masking.py:179-224.
- [C1] Nonfrontal unwarped-box mask routing default '0' (off), 'auto' routes by pose, '1' always: app/roop/procmgr_masking.py:95-117.
- [E1b] globals defaults: face_detector_threshold 0.50 (comment: lowered from 0.60 for slight angles), face_detector_nms 0.40, face_detector_size '640', detector_scale_pyramid 'auto', detector_padding_border 64 px, detector_padding_mode 'reflect', adaptive_detection True, adaptive_detect_interval 8, adaptive_motion_threshold 0.05, adaptive_roi_rescue True, distance_threshold 1, no_face_action 1, track_identities True, sparse_auto_range False, temporal_detection False, identity_lock False (margin 0.08, hold 3) (globals.py:~66-122,150,230-240).

This is 2D similarity only, never 3D.
- [E1b] core.AdaptiveLODDispatcher: LOD0_THRESHOLD 120.0, LOD1_THRESHOLD 350.0 on face bbox diagonal D (core.py:~1225-1226,1138-1200): D<120 -> LOD0 swap_size 128; 120<=D<=350 -> LOD1 swap_size 256, gpen_size 256; D>350 -> LOD2 swap_size 512, gpen_size 512. Dispatch is by diagonal only (no angle input).

Heuristic thresholds.
- [E1b] advanced_settings_manager AUTO: face_detector_threshold 0.45 when 4K or >=3 faces, else 0.5; detector_scale_pyramid "on" if >5 faces else "auto" (advanced_settings_manager.py:~1015-1025). FAST preset: detector scrfd_2.5g, threshold 0.55, pyramid off, temporal_step 2 (:806-826).
- [E1b] Quality endpoint: detection_rate % and sharpness via Laplacian var (/800 -> score) (routes_quality.py:~120-130).
- [C2] Detection/pose: temporal_tracker full-frame detect every 8 frames (app/roop/temporal_tracker.py:299), ROI pad stable 0.85 / uncertain 1.35, min ROI 160px (303-305); pre-pass interval K clamp 1..8 default 1 (app/roop/prepass_policy.py:23-26). State machine labels profile at yaw >55Â° (app/roop/temporal_state_machine.py:365). Stage0 pose bins yaw Â±25 / pitch Â±15 / roll Â±15 (app/roop/stage0_preflight.py:63-72 comments) â†’ 3x3x3 source cells. visibility_alignment rebuilds primary keypoints with visibility <0.50 (app/roop/visibility_alignment.py:41). Association: composite_cost = identityÂ·reliabilityÂ·(1-sim) + 1Â·(1-GIoU) + 0.5Â·kp-dist/size (app/roop/association_cost.py:139-166); tracker.py uses max_cost .85, 0.6 motion + 0.4 identity (app/roop/tracker.py:250-253).

### (2) 2D warping vs 3D volumetric / faceset / .fsz
- [F] Pipeline is 2D: InsightFace 5-pt similarity alignment (`estimate_norm(kps,112)` in Frame Editor tracked-drawing, `app/ui/tabs/extras_tab.py:927-945`). The only "3D" feature is `use_3d_recon` "ðŸ§Š 3D source pose matching (experimental)": "Warps the source face to approximate the target head pose before embedding"; uses only insightface `landmark_3d_68` + solvePnP/warpAffine, no volumetric model, no model download (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:78-83`, `app/setup_3drecon.py:1-24`). Default True in app/default_config.yaml:161 but False in settings.schema.json (use_3d_recon).
- [F] "Multi-angle source bank": with multi-image faceset, auto-select source whose pose best matches target frame; needs `.fsz` with front+profile photos (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:84-89`); default True in yaml:164, False in schema.
- [F] `.fsz` = plain zip of PNG face crops (`app/ui/tabs/facemgr_tab.py:174-188` â†’ `util.zip`, output `faceset.fsz`); no 3D data in the format. Faceset upload cap 512 MiB Ã— 64 files; `.fsz` only ext (`app/safe_paths.py:148,153-158`).
- [F] Frontalization: controls exist but are `visible=False, interactive=False`, value False, threshold slider default 30.0 (range 0â€“90) (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:90-103`); config default `frontalization_threshold 15`, `use_frontalization false` (yaml:39,162). Dead in legacy UI.
- [F] Swap-model dropdown is hard-coded `choices=["inswapper"]`, hidden, non-interactive (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:104-110`) although the backend/config use `hyperswap`/`realswap`; `start_swap` default `swap_model='inswapper'` (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:2299-2300`).
- [F] Models benchmarked in `bench_stages.py` swap stage: inswapper_128 (128â†’4 tiles @256), reswapper_256, hyperswap_1a/1b/1c_256, ghost_1/3_256, simswap_256, simswap_unofficial_512, blendswap_256, uniface_256, hififace_unofficial_256 (`app/tools/bench_stages.py:80-92`). Pixel-boost tiles = (subsample/output_size)Â²; per-face cost = tiles Ã— ms/call.
- [E1a] 3D/volumetric: `use_3d_recon` False, `use_source_bank` False, `use_frontalization` False, stereo_mode "MONO_2D", vr_mode False (app/settings.py:1157-1162; schema). No .fsz/faceset volumetric logic in group; faceset routes are in routes_faceset (outside). Pipeline is 2D inswapper-style (`swap_model` default 'realswap', app/settings.py:1163).
- [D] Entire group is 2D: crops are 128/256/512 fixed (app/roop/vram_governor.py:94-112 swap 128, GPEN 256/512/1024/2048, wav2lip 96, musetalk 256); no 3D volumetric / faceset / .fsz code. 1k3d68 appears only as a 68-pt landmark ONNX forced to FP32 (app/roop/precision_policy.py:458). `GpuFaceSwapStage` (app/roop/vectorized_pipeline.py:756) does affine align/reverse-warp on CUDA, but is unwired. LivePortrait warping_spade 5-D GridSample is rewritten into 4-D ops for TRT (app/roop/gridsample5d.py:1-17,238-300).
- [B] Pipeline is purely 2D: 5-pt similarity warp into model crop, swap, inverse-affine paste (BaseFaceSwapper.paste_back/paste_roi; app/roop/processors/frame/swapper_base.py:181-349). No depth, mesh, NeRF, Gaussian splat or .fsz handling in group B.
- [B] Faceset: V2 faceset provides pose-binned embeddings: `pose_embedding_for_target` selects exact (yaw,pitch) cell with fallback=False else identity centroid â€” app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:22-70. Dermal patch from V2 faceset used in legacy swapper (app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:1406-1467, resolve_dermal_patch).
- [B] Profile/lateral views: no 3D reconstruction; the code measured that no swapper can rescue a track that never bound a source ("limiter is intake, not the net"), swap rate identical 52.1%/94.8% across models â€” app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:143-168.
- [B] Identity-algebra direction assets (npz+json): only 'jawline' marked writable and weak (jaw ratio -0.011..+0.009); age, gender, expression NOT writable â€” app/roop/assets/identity_render_validation.json:13,47,81,116.
- [B] Swap model resolutions: inswapper_128 (128, [0,1], emap, B=1 only), reswapper_128/256, hyperswap_1a/1b/1c 256 and 1a/1b/1c 512, alpha_256, ghost_1/2/3 256, simswap_256, simswap_unofficial_512 (512), blendswap_256, uniface_256, hififace_unofficial_256, InStyleSwapper256 A/B/C fp16, cscs_256 (3 files, 1.26 GB), realswap = hyperswap_1a_256 + secondary hififace â€” app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:116-536.
- [E2] Everything in E2 is strictly 2D: live tracked frames are a 2D similarity (partial affine) warp of the previous composite output + hull mask blended with alpha, live_mode.py:~280-330. No volumetric/.fsz/faceset code in this group. `.roop` is JSON session (format `roop-session`, v1), app/roop/project_io.py:23-24; face_bank stored as embeddings list + clusters only, app/roop/project_io.py:117-125.
- [A] NO volumetric/NeRF/mesh/Gaussian/depth data exists in group A. 'face_3d_recon' is PnP + horizontal flip + bounded shear (yaw shear cap 0.20, pitch cap 0.08) on a 2D source crop (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:172-220,250-263); `Face3DRecon.fit_source` returns the crop only and `render_from_coefficients` returns None (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:345-355). Default use_3d_recon False (globals.py:350; app/roop/quality_profiles.py:131 enables it in one profile).
- [A] face_frontalize = global 2x3 affine (cv2.estimateAffine2D RANSAC thr 8, 2000 iters) from 68-pt reprojection at zero rotation; rejects reflection det<=0 (app/roop/face_frontalize.py:123-141). Trigger >25 deg yaw or pitch (174-191).
- [A] .fsz format (faceset_v2.py): ZIP of root-level PNG members `0.png,1.png,...` (legacy contract) + `metadata.json` (FORMAT_NAME 'roop.fsz', version 2); members written with fixed ZipInfo date 1980-01-01, deflate level 6 (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:956-1010); atomic temp-file write; SHA-256 per PNG in metadata.integrity (994, 1129-1154); read verifies zip CRC + hashes; legacy archive (no metadata) is accepted and re-detected (app/roop/faceset_manager.py:209-212). `migrate_legacy_fsz` stamps all entries pose_bin 'frontal' without detection (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:1164-1200).
- [A] metadata fields: schema, version, source_name, compatibility.legacy_fields_preserved [faces, ref_images, embedding_average, embeddings_backup, face_3d, face_3d_bank, face_poses], identity{arcface_embedding, embedding, normalized_embedding, mean_embedding, aggregation 'quality_weighted_mean_of_pose_specific_embeddings'}, identity_details (16x16 descriptor + high_frequency 64px residual), default_embedding, mean_embedding, pose_bins (3x3 yaw x pitch cells with embedding/members/support), dermal_patch, pose_bank (9-way), sources[] (per-reference: identity.{512-d raw+normalized}, geometry.{bbox, landmarks_68, landmarks_68_3d, landmarks_2d, yaw/pitch/roll, face_scale, facial_proportions}, quality.{9 parts+score}, appearance, expression, identity_details, pose_bin, pose_cell), rejected[], gates{laplacian_floor 100, min_identity_cosine .60, min_quality .35}, index{embedding_metric 'cosine_distance', normalized_embeddings, reference_members} (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:906-948,554-587).
- [A] 3D data in .fsz: only `landmarks_68_3d` = insightface landmark_3d_68 xyz (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:199-203,566). No mesh/depth/SH/texture-atlas. `face_3d`/`face_3d_bank` are in-memory crop caches (app/roop/FaceSet.py:18-22) listed as legacy fields but not written by write_faceset_v2 [INFERENCE from absence in the metadata builder].
- [A] Selection caps: DEFAULT_MAX_ENTRIES 32, MAX_PER_BIN 6 (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:42-43). Quality weights: face_size .18, sharpness .20, det .16, lm .12, exposure .10, saturation .05, occlusion .10 (default 0.75 when not supplied), pose_suitability .09 (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:550-553,535-536).
- [A] Load path re-detects each reference PNG and matches cached rows by bbox IoU; geometry/embedding cache is NOT used for the live Face (app/source_gallery.py:259-301). `faceset_manager` returns cached normalized embeddings only for the 'default' model and is unwired (app/roop/faceset_manager.py:24-26).
- [A] Pose-aware source choice: (a) V1/folder: `reference_embedding_for_pose` nearest-two blend (app/roop/face_reference.py:269-299; blend only if second dist^2 within 100 of first); (b) V2: `pose_bin_embedding` 3x3 cell all-or-nothing else default (app/roop/FaceSet.py:168-181); (c) `get_pose_matched_embedding` top-3 inverse-distance SLERP, torch (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:1264-1384); (d) `select_reference_index` cost .60 cosine +.25 yaw/45 +.10 pitch/35 +.05 lum (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:1387-1414); (e) `pose_source_selector.select_pose_aware_source` temporal cost with hysteresis margin .035 (app/roop/pose_source_selector.py:306-396); (f) `source_portfolio.route` e=normalise(.7*e_profile+.3*e_fused) when |yaw|>35 and profile bin exists (app/roop/source_portfolio.py:326-336).
- [A] `needs_3d` (app/roop/pose_source_selector.py:342-396) set True when reason != 'source_pose_sufficient' (pose_gap>.42, between poses >.12 gap & >10 deg, off_axis>=72, proportion mismatch>.42, perspective risk>.72, inverted, conf<.45); consumer app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:6051-6052 gates use_3d_recon path, i.e. flip/shear only.
- [C1] 2D similarity alignment only: `canonicalize_face_alignment(plate, target_face, subsample_size, swap_template)` (app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5731); template arcface / mtcnn_512 (hififace) / arcface_112 (ghost/simswap): app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5726-5730. model_output_size default 128 (inswapper), subsample_size multiple of it => pixel boost tiles `(subsample/128)^2`: app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5718-5724.
- [C1] Pixel boost = slice aligned crop into model-sized tiles, run swap per tile, reassemble (implode/explode): app/roop/procmgr_tiling.py:103-110.
- [C1] "3D" in this repo is only pose-based: `use_3d_recon` option, source bank (multi-angle), frontalization; no volumetric/faceset(.fsz) code in this group (grep). Jaw reshape = 106-pt TPS (indices 0..32) liquify via cv2.remap, off unless enabled: app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:262-292.
- [E1b] This group contains only 2D similarity/affine paths (cuda_warp_affine grid_sample bilinear, app/roop/utilities.py:162-215; rotation_affines; profile_alignment_matrix). `globals.use_3d_recon = False` flag exists (globals.py:~335) and core.batch_process_regular accepts `use_3d_recon=False` (app/roop/core.py:1437) â€“ consumer outside group; [INFERENCE] a legacy toggle, no volumetric/.fsz/faceset 3D logic in these 31 files. Facesets referenced only as `INPUT_FACESETS` list (globals.py:323) and `VideoJob.facesets` tuple (app/roop/process_manager.py:33).
- [C2] 2D vs 3D: nothing here is volumetric. light_harmonizer builds a hard-coded ellipsoid+Gaussian depth prior (app/roop/light_harmonizer.py:65-150), not a recon; quality_profiles "ultra" sets `use_3d_recon: True` (quality_profiles.py:~110) but only toggles a flag. vfx_multipass is 2x3 affine â†’ 3x3 + corner pin (app/roop/vfx_multipass.py:22-45). Faceset/.fsz not in this group (aside from final_quality_gate.audit_faceset 134).

### (3) Visual quality / blending / masks / color / restorers / resolution
- [F] Legacy UI controls (name Â· default Â· range): Offset Face Top/Bottom/Left/Right Â· cfg.mask_* (yaml 0) Â· 0â€“2.0 step 0.01; Face Mask Edge Blend Â· yaml 25 Â· 0â€“200; Mouth Mask Top/Bottom/Left/Right Â· 1 Â· 0â€“2.0; Mouth Mask Edge Blend Â· yaml 10 Â· 0â€“200; mask engine dropdown ["None","Clip2Seg","DFL XSeg"] (yaml default `DFL XSeg`); clip text default `cup,hands,hair,banana`; "Restore original mouth area" checkbox; Original/Enhanced blend ratio 0â€“1 (yaml 1); subsample upscale ["128px","256px","512px"] (yaml 256px); number of swapping steps 1â€“5 (yaml 1) (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:66-168,245-255`; `app/default_config.yaml:35,49-56,63,68,130,72`).
- [F] Enhancer dropdown (legacy): None, Codeformer, DMDNet, GFPGAN, GPEN 256 Pro, GPEN, GPEN Ultimate, Restoreformer++, Restore Ultra (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:247`); yaml default `Restore Ultra`; schema default `UltraMax`, which is NOT in the legacy dropdown (schema `selected_enhancer`). Mask engines in schema: default `RealityUX` not in legacy dropdown.

PauseController cooperative pause at output-safe boundary: runtime.py:42; ProcessMgr._checkpoint_at_safe_output (1613).
- [C1] Source IO: cv2.VideoCapture with NVDEC/ffmpeg fallback (open_video_capture), animated WebP in memory, HDR managed path, VFR timestamps kept: batch.py:385-430,704. endframe exclusive: batch.py:410. Audio via util_ffmpeg.extract_audio_wav: batch.py:141. Sparse auto-range keyframe prescan (`sparse_auto_range`) for active ranges: batch.py:842-929. Stab chunk RAM caps 8192/4096/1536 MB by total RAM (>=55000/>=28000/else), share 0.75|0.58|0.40: stabilization.py:150-213.
- [E1b] **Queue model** (routes_queue.py): persisted to `queue.json` next to module (:93), atomic write via `.tmp`+`os.replace` (:227-240). JOB_STATES = QUEUED, PREPARING, PROCESSING, PAUSE_REQUESTED, PAUSED, COMPLETED, FAILED, CANCELLED, INTERRUPTED, RECOVERABLE (:97-100); SCHEMA_VERSION 2 (:101); legacy `status` projection pending/running/finished/failed/stopped (:102-113); RUNNABLE_STATES = QUEUED, RECOVERABLE (:121). Runner flags (running/paused/current) are NOT persisted so queue always restarts idle (:227-229). On load, any PREPARING/PROCESSING/PAUSE_REQUESTED/PAUSED (or legacy 'running') job becomes RECOVERABLE with error "interrupted by an app restart; start the queue to retry" + interrupted_at (:204-214); load() then also rewrites any remaining status=='running' to pending (:249-255; effectively dead after normalisation).
- [E1b] Concurrency: ONE runner thread "queue-runner" (daemon), `_generation` token retires stale runners (:1044-1068); strictly sequential, RLock `_lock`. start refuses with 400 (nothing runnable), 409 (single run processing), 409 (benchmark running) (:1046-1062).
- [E1b] Job record: schema_version, id (uuid hex[:12]), target_name, target_media_id, source_index, source_name, source_id, payload, processing_selection (frozen), selection_version, request_id, frame_start/frame_end, segment {group,index,count}, label, status, error, added, started, finished, state, progress{fraction,frames_done,frames_total,fps,eta_s,phase,updated_at}, outputs[], cancel_requested, recoverable, project_id (:346-380). Validation: source_index>=0 int, frame_start/end non-negative ints with end>=start (:382-410). Segment marker requires index in 1..count and count>=2 (:332-343).
- [E1b] Dispatch (_run_one@736): re-validates processing_selection after activating target; project jobs validated via `_validate_project` -> RECOVERABLE with "cannot safely resume: â€¦"; fresh jobs create a project checkpoint (`_create_project`) -> FAILED "could not create processing checkpoint" on error (:840-870); state PROCESSING persisted (:~873); exposes `roop_globals._segment_spec` and `_dashboard_job{job_id,batch_index,batch_count}` (:~880-895). Auto-fallback: if payload/job `auto_fallback` and run errored, retried once with enhancer "None" (:~897-905). Segment custody audit on COMPLETED segments + auto-join when group done (:1010-1030).
- [E1b] Cancel: queued job -> CANCELLED immediately ("cancelled before processing"); current job -> cooperative via `_cancel_requested` + `_stop_current()`; 409 if already completed/failed/cancelled (:1193-1220). `/stop` ends batch and stops current render (:1405-1424).
- [E1b] Resume: `/api/queue/resume` with `project_id` validates checkpoint, verifies segments (`roop.journal.verify_and_recover_segments(..., discard_truncated_tail=True)`), else RECOVERABLE (:1096-1180; projects route :253-288). stage_cache gives Stage1/2 resume (see above). Queue file lives in source tree, not user data dir (:93) â€” potential permission/portability issue.
- [E1b] Output serving: HTTP Range 206/416 handling, 1 MiB chunks, ETag/version validators, share-delete file handle on Windows (app/routes_output.py:168-300).
- [E1b] FFmpeg: `cfr_video_filter` = `fps=<r>,setpts=N/(<r>*TB)`; CFR fps rejected (->fallback 24.0) if non-finite, <=0 or >240 (app/roop/utilities.py:558-575); ffprobe audio sample-rate probe timeout 10 s (:577-588). Export presets: reel_9x16 1080x1920, portrait_4x5 1080x1350, square_1x1 1080x1080, landscape_16x9 1920x1080; never overwrites (suffix `_{preset}_{n}`) (app/routes_export.py:21-65). extras video path writes mp4v via cv2.VideoWriter then `finalize_web_video` (routes_extras.py:~95-110). core.ensure_video_encoder probes configured encoder, picks fallback via qsv_encode.choose_encoder or aborts (app/roop/core.py:2066-2085); destroy() waits up to 120 s for the encode thread to finalize the file (app/roop/core.py:2099-2135).
- [C2] Queue/video IO: render_policy fixed flags (app/roop/render_policy.py:24-31); hdr_pipeline ffmpeg pipe yuv444p16le read, p010 HEVC/AV1 NVENC encode with 3-frame probe, NVDEC unless 12-bit/422/444 (app/roop/hdr_pipeline.py:405-479,614-623); timeline_tracks audio envelope via ffmpeg PCM (app/roop/timeline_tracks.py:203-265); trajectory_cache persisted to checkpoint, mmap npy (app/roop/trajectory_cache.py:1-47); vfx_multipass 10-bit beauty+matte+crops; preview_provider 2 s deadline, 32 frame LRU.

## Gaps / stubs / defects found
Per reader group; items tagged `[INFERENCE]` were not confirmed by execution.

### [A] detect/pose/identity/faceset
- face_3d_recon.Face3DRecon.fit_source returns only the crop and render_from_coefficients returns None 'kept so ProcessMgr references don't break' (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:340-355): stubs; the feature named '3D recon' is 2D flip/shear. Docstring says 'no additional model needed' (app/roop/face_3d_recon.py:30). There is NO volumetric/3D face model, NeRF, mesh, FLAME/3DMM or depth anywhere in group A.
- Pose convention hazards: solve_pose_5pt returns (yaw, pitch, roll) but app/roop/face_bank.py:244,359 unpacks `pitch, yaw, roll = solve_pose_5pt(kps)` and stores 'yaw': yaw / 'pitch': pitch -> yaw and pitch are SWAPPED in Face Bank entries (offaxis_deg is symmetric so off_axis is unaffected). routes_facemgr._facemgr_ingest treats insightface `face.pose` as (yaw,pitch,roll) (app/routes_facemgr.py:131-132) though insightface order is (pitch,yaw,roll) (app/roop/head_pose.py:53-54; app/roop/faceset_v2.py:219-223 converts correctly) -> when landmark_3d_68 pose exists, Face Manager radar/sharpness FIQA pose and written yaw/pitch are permuted. FaceSet.ensure_face_poses uses a different EPnP decomposer (frontal ~+/-170) vs solve_pose_5pt (app/roop/FaceSet.py:250-256 docs); app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:16-18 itself notes prose in the tree contradicting the sign convention (face_3d_recon says pitch+ = up, nonfrontal says +30 = tilted up).
- face_analyser.adaptive_alignment_matrix / profile_stable_anchor_alignment implement a 3-point profile solver but canonicalize_face_alignment explicitly replaced profile templates with plain `estimate_norm` because they doubled features (app/roop/face_analyser.py:699-722); profile_aware_umeyama_alignment is still used by app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:1954 -> two alignment policies coexist.
- face_util _rescue_upscaled is gated by rescue_small_faces default False (globals.py:287): small-face rescue is off by default; first-pass detector at 640 (forced for scrfd/r50) can miss small faces in 4K.
- Detector thresholds: global default face_detector_threshold 0.50 (globals.py:59) vs fallback 0.60 hard-coded in analyser prepare (app/roop/face_util.py:267) vs capture_seed MIN_DET_SCORE 0.6 vs face_bank min_det_score .55 vs adaptive min_confidence .55 vs sparse scan floor .25 (many inconsistent hard-coded floors).
- geometric_alignment.py: module-level `from face_engine.pipeline.stereo import ...` is a hard import of an out-of-tree/optional package (app/roop/geometric_alignment.py:630); module only used by hyperswap_optimizer; `LandmarkGeometrySanity.validate_landmarks` inverted-axis branch is `pass` (dead check, app/roop/geometric_alignment.py:85-88).
- adaptive_detector/face_analyser.TemporalFaceDetector are two overlapping temporal-detection schedulers (adaptive: interval 8, coast 4; temporal: interval 5, max_age 15) with different scene-cut hist implementations; [INFERENCE] only one is wired per path.
- Recognition selection is NOT wired to live identity matching (ui_recognition.SCOPE_NOTE; app/roop/face_analyser.py:1497-1510; app/roop/faceset_manager.py:24-26 'no render consumer yet'). AdaFace off by default (ROOP_ADAFACE) with uncalibrated 0.5 cosine-distance threshold (app/roop/recognizer_adaface.py:21-27,66). Swapper hard-locked to 512-d w600k (swap_identity.py).
- faceset V2 does not cache usable runtime geometry: loader re-detects every PNG (app/source_gallery.py:259-301), so V2 'cached analysis' only helps selection metadata, not load cost; metadata occlusion defaults 0.75 when no occlusion given (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:535-536) so quality score has a built-in constant.
- .fsz has no embedding-model tag (identity.embedding_model absent in written archives; app/roop/faceset_manager.py:21-22) and no 3D mesh/texture; migrate_legacy_fsz stamps every entry 'frontal' (app/roop/faceset_v2.py:1187) without detection => legacy pose bank is fictional until re-saved.
- landmark_refresh default OFF (app/roop/landmark_refresh.py:45) despite measured improvement (median 1.21%->0.58% IOD error).
- routes_facemgr: module-global singleton state (not per-session/multi-user); build silently falls back to legacy zip if V2 writer raises (app/routes_facemgr.py:383-390), losing metadata; _facemgr_ingest swallows pose-set errors to zeros (136-141).
- source_gallery.estimate_face_pose_from_kps uses crude ratio thresholds (.65/1.55 yaw, .65/1.45 pitch) while the rest of the tree uses solve_pose_5pt: gallery pose labels can disagree with swap-time routing.
- face_bank.py solve_pose_5pt(kps) may return None -> tuple-unpack TypeError (app/roop/face_bank.py:244,359) (solve_pose_5pt returns None on degenerate input, app/roop/face_util.py:2955-2961).
- Hard-coded limits that fail silently: yoloface fixed 640 (guard added after measured 0% recall at 512, app/roop/yoloface.py:62-95); r50 static TRT profile ignores det_size (app/roop/retinaface.py:56-62); faceset MAX_MEMBER_BYTES 64 MiB skip (app/roop/faceset_manager.py:42); scan step widening at 9000 frames; angle shortlist 40/bin and gaps 10-deg cells cap rare angles at 10 frames (app/roop/angle_portfolio.py:342).
- Identity thresholds differ by module: 0.65 (face_distance_threshold default cosine DISTANCE, app/roop/face_analyser.py:795-796), 0.65 DEFAULT_SIMILARITY (cosine similarity, app/roop/scanner.py:44), 0.65 DEFAULT_MIN_COSINE reference clustering (app/roop/face_reference.py:27), .62/.50 dual (similarity), identity_manager gate 0.45 (cost), bystander .42/.22/.12, faceset_v2 .60 outlier, identity_algebra .80 guard; units (distance vs similarity) mixed -> mis-tuning risk (docstring app/roop/face_analyser.py:777-785 explicitly warns).


### [B] swap/enhancers/masks
- **POST /api/enhancer/ultramax/compile is simulated**: _compile_engine_task only asyncio.sleeps (0.3+0.5+0.3 s) and publishes a fake engine_path models/trt_cache/ultramax_<prec>_<prov>.plan; docstring says "Simulate or execute" â€” app/roop/api/enhancer_routes.py:420-460. Initial compilation state is hard-set to ready/1.0 (app/roop/api/enhancer_routes.py:237).
- **tile_diffusion.py "latent diffusion" is not diffusion**: builtin path does bilinear 8x down, 4th channel = RGB mean, Laplacian update with alphas [0.85,0.5,0.2,0.05]*0.12, bicubic up, 0.25/0.75 mix; CPU path is Gaussian(1.5)+Laplacian boost 1.35. No UNet/VAE. Real pipeline only if app/models/diffusion/<sd-turbo> exists (diffusers). Docs claim "few-step latent diffusion", "2-6 FPS" â€” app/roop/tile_diffusion.py:419-470.
- **Wav2Lip falls back to a hand-drawn ellipse mouth** (cavity/teeth/tongue ellipses) when ONNX fails; comment says input [-1,1] but code uses /255 [0,1]; model URL github.com/primefaces/roop-models (unverified host) â€” app/roop/processors/Lipsync_Wav2Lip.py:24,175-262.
- **Legacy frame/face_swapper.py** (2269 lines, EAR/mouth/SH/grain) is not on the render path (only tests + scene_detector.clear_temporal_state import it); has silent no-op fallback stubs for pose/Umeyama/CUDA warp (return 0.0/None/identity) â€” app/roop/processors/frame/face_swapper.py:255-292. Docstring EAR<0.21 vs const 0.18.
- processors/face_swapper.py (TensorRTFaceSwapper), processors/face_enhancer.py (GPENEnhancer) and processors/frame/temporal_stabilizer + color_matcher have no production importers outside onnx_swappers/tests; GPENEnhancer/TRT swapper raise RuntimeError without CUDA.
- hififace verify_tol=0.65 calibrated on one synthetic clip; self-declared "re-measure on real footage" â€” app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:231-233.
- Measured negative results shipped as options: CSCS/InStyleSwapper worse than realswap on real video; only identity intake limits profile swaps â€” app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:96-168.
- hyperswap_optimizer.get_vram_telemetry invents defaults total 12288/free 8192 MB when NVML/torch unavailable (app/roop/hyperswap_optimizer.py:138-142); NVML init at import.
- MAR 5-pt fallback returns constant 0.10 (below 0.18 trigger so mouth restore never fires on 5-pt-only faces) â€” app/roop/enhancers/mouth_dynamics.py:124-125.
- Enhance_DMDNet loads './models/DMDNet.pth' relative to CWD (not resolve_relative_path) â€” app/roop/processors/Enhance_DMDNet.py:275; module-level torch/torchvision imports hard-require torch.
- Enhance_KEEP is EXPERIMENTAL and silently passes frames through when sidecar missing â€” app/roop/processors/Enhance_KEEP.py:10-12.
- xseg3_optimizer docstring names ConfidenceAwareMaskRefiner class but code has function refine_xseg3_mask (docs/code drift) â€” app/roop/xseg3_optimizer.py:11 vs 141.
- vr_util.get_perspective destructures img.shape as [orig_width, orig_height] (actually h,w) â€” app/roop/vr_util.py:12.
- model_registry omits inswapper, ghost, simswap etc. from DEFAULT_REGISTRY (only hififace, hyperswap*, alpha, reswapper*): those fall through to FaceSwapInsightFace.SWAP_MODELS legacy path â€” app/roop/processors/frame/model_registry.py:141-165.
- Duplicate keys in SWAP_MODELS (hyperswap/hyperswap_1a/hyperswap_256 -> same file; reswapper/reswapper_256) â€” app/roop/processors/FaceSwapInsightFace.py:133-247.
- Hard limits: Frame_Filter C64 fixed 320x200; frame_masking fixed 1024; TemporalMatrixStabilizer max_gap=1 resets track on any skipped frame (round-robin workers); ModelRegistry unknown name -> FALLBACK_SWAP_MODEL 'inswapper' (128px).


### [C1] ProcessMgr+procmgr
- No NotImplementedError/TODO/stub in this group (grep for stub|placeholder|NotImplemented returned zero hits).
- 3D is not present: pose-gated heuristics only (frontalization, source bank, jaw TPS); no volumetric/faceset path.
- Several features are measured-negative and kept OFF by default: bystander veto, flow-align, hi-res miss redetect, ROI-crop, single-person veto (0), non-frontal mask routing (0), mask fusion, occlusion fast-restore.
- Doc/code tension: process_face comment documents a reverted refusal of synthetic (gap-filled/coasted) faces; synthetic faces are now swapped (app/roop/ProcessMgr.py:5601-5620) â€” can paint onto background if coast guards miss.
- Hard-coded: STALE=15, IOU_MIN=0.2 are literals not env (tracking.py:428); stitching/veto numbers tuned on a few clips (comments cite d6, Love/Weeds, Taras).
- Scene-cut thresholds are not in this group (delegated to roop.scene_detector) [INFERENCE: none verified here].
- ProcessMgr.py is a 7654-line monolith (process_face ~2000 lines) with `_swallowed` broad except fallbacks throughout (many silent degrade paths).
- Config coupling via roop.globals getattr defaults and ~60 env knobs makes defaults hard to audit.


### [C2] temporal/masks/color/tracking
- light_harmonizer docstring says "Neural environment re-lighting" (app/roop/light_harmonizer.py:1) but implementation is classical (ellipsoid prior + bilateral + LS light fit); no neural model, no real 3D.
- quality_profiles "ultra" 3D recon is just a settings flag `use_3d_recon` (quality_profiles.py:~110); profile names `hyperswap`/`realswap`/`RealityUX` are labels mapped to settings, not verified here.
- Kalman kps stabilizer is not default and measured no better than One-Euro on real footage (app/roop/one_euro.py:395-412 docstring); defaults tuned on a single static clip.
- Two divergent scene-cut implementations with different defaults (ratio 6.0/window 61/cold .16 in one_euro.py vs 4.5/45/.15 in scene_detector.py) â€” duplicate heuristics, both histogram-based (2D appearance only).
- Opt-in modules default OFF: temporal_identity, temporal_occlusion, temporal_compositing, temporal_quality (see rows) â€” quality depends on config enabling them.
- final_quality_gate/stage17_audit/regression_audit are contracts/documentation: no measured gate thresholds; PREVIOUS_PHASES all `quality_status: evidence_pending` (app/roop/final_quality_gate.py:63-72); stage17 baselines come from SESSION_LOGS.md text, not computed.
- trajectory_cache explicitly does not feed the swap alignment (docstring 1-47): smoothed 112px matrices only for HUD/checkpoint.
- EpipolarStereoTracker (app/roop/temporal_state_machine.py:877) delegates to an external coupler and is only meaningful for stereo input.
- Hard-coded limits: tracker max_lost 30 / coast 15; state machine travel 45px/frame (fails on very fast low-res motion); MAX_SUBSTITUTED 8 frames in flow chain; roto MAX_POLYGONS 24 / points 512; mouth audio offset Â±500ms; motion blur kernel â‰¤31; trajectory series points â‰¤5000.
- NVOF backend unverifiable in tests (`pragma: no cover`, app/roop/flow_backend.py:214).
- sam2_runs.py is only range arithmetic (no SAM2 model code in this file).


### [D] compute/runtime
- **Dead gate in optimizer**: `SearchSpace.cudnn_probe_state` looks for `cudnn_algo.lowered_models/downgraded_models/unsafe_models` (app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:696-712) but cudnn_algo.py defines none of them (symbols: record, known_algo, apply_algo, has_cuda_ep, probe), so the cuDNN axis is always reported "healthy" and the guard against re-enabling a rejected mode is inoperative.
- **session_pool docs vs code**: module header says pooling is opt-in "default unset keeps single session byte-for-byte" (app/roop/session_pool.py:12-15) but `_auto_pool_defaults` returns 2/2 (12 GB) and 4/4 (>=15.5 GB) automatically (:100-103); docstring table says ">= 15.5 GB -> 8/8" (:82) while code returns 4,4.
- **precision_policy comment contradiction**: header says INT8/FP8 "represented ... a TensorRT feature flag alone does not enable either" (app/roop/precision_policy.py:39-41) yet `_IMPLEMENTED_PROVIDER_PRECISIONS={'bf16','fp8'}` (:42) lets FP8 through for face_swap; runtime_optimizer.PrecisionSelector rejects anything but bf16 (app/roop/runtime_optimizer.py:1207-1215) so the two layers disagree on FP8.
- **Swapper FP16 contradiction** (see axis 3): policy says safe/candidate, bench + predictor say FP32-forced.
- **runtime_state._surgeon_info hard-codes** `surgical_models=[xseg,2d106det,1k3d68,restoreformer_pp,codeformer]` (runtime_state.py:~345) while PRECISION_OVERRIDES forces xseg/2d106det/1k3d68/RF++ to plain fp32 and the surgeon is invoked only by face_engine/core/trt_compiler.py:278 â€” UI/diagnostics imply surgical mixed precision is active when the ORT render path does not use it.
- **Unwired modules** (code present, no render caller): fused_postprocess.py (docstring "Not wired into the render"), l2_persistence.py, vectorized_pipeline.py (+hardware_streamer ring, optimized_trt_engine, RetinaFaceDecoder, GpuFaceSwapStage), inference_optimizer.py, unified_runtime_scheduler.py, trt_engine.py, trt_session_builder (only optimized_*/verify), pipeline_concurrency (only an _ACTIVE_ENGINE probe in app/roop/api/enhancer_routes.py:385), worker_pool.py ("NO RENDER USES THIS"), optimized_prepass (live path is procmgr_tracking).
- **Synthetic/simulated benchmarks**: inference_optimizer.benchmark_mode fabricates latencies (CPU x3.5, TRT FP16 x0.55 + 1.2 ms dispatch for small models, sin-jitter, vram 128.0 MB constant, notes "Light/Synthesized benchmark profile") when the live path is unavailable (app/roop/inference_optimizer.py:735-760); pipeline_concurrency benchmarks MockStageExecutor, not real stages (app/roop/pipeline_concurrency.py:355-490); bench.py feeds synthetic tensors and models detect post-processing as constants (app/roop/bench.py:36-43).
- **Inconsistent VRAM ceilings across modules**: governor 90% ceiling (app/roop/vram_governor.py:59), runtime fraction .90, optimizer admission .82 / balanced .80 (app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:122-124), BottleneckAnalyzer VRAM-bound 85% (app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:1079), scheduler margin .12 (=88%), unified_runtime_scheduler 4070 safe 9728/12288 (79%) and 3060 4915/6144 (80%) (app/roop/unified_runtime_scheduler.py:176-196). Different layers may admit/reject the same state.
- **Hard-coded two-GPU assumptions**: pipeline_concurrency profiles (RTX 4070 12 GB / 3060 6 GB), unified_runtime_scheduler HardwareCapacity (12288 / 6144 MB, 24/8 cores), inference_optimizer name match "3060"/"laptop"/"4070"/"ada" (app/roop/inference_optimizer.py:166-170); hardware_validation accepts only the two named targets (app/roop/hardware_validation.py:18). A 3050/4060/5090 maps to generic buckets only by VRAM.
- **TRT cache location mismatch**: strict builder defaults to relative `models/trt_cache` from CWD (app/roop/trt_session_builder.py:334,375) while routes_trt_cache clears `app/models/trt_cache` (app/routes_trt_cache.py:45-46) and vectorized/verify use `models/trt_cache`; engines built from another CWD are invisible to the cache UI and survive 'clear'.
- **Sub-7 GB hard limits**: pools forced to 0, explicit pool values rejected (app/roop/session_pool.py:176-209); batch 1, in-flight 1 (app/roop/runtime_optimizer.py:1648-1660); NVDEC disabled in pipeline_concurrency 3060 profile (app/roop/pipeline_concurrency.py:243) but nvdec_reader merely drops prefetch to 1 (app/roop/nvdec_reader.py:190-197); inconsistent.
- **OpenVINO untested on target machines** per docstring (app/roop/openvino_runner.py:17-20); int8 requires >=8 real recorded samples at the dominant shape, no synthetic path.
- **INT8 never selected via ORT**: int8/fp8 flags are probed but policy forces fp32 (app/roop/precision_policy.py:397-400); INT8/FP8 only exist in the separate native trt_quant path for the swapper, which needs a calibration set built by tools/build_calibration_set.py else raises CalibrationSetMissing and falls back (app/roop/trt_quant.py:35-39).
- **Heuristic fallbacks**: _fallback_landmarks from fixed bounding-box fractions (app/roop/optimized_prepass.py:206-226); quality_metrics profile/occlusion judged from synthetic ground truth flags; regression changed-face metric = MAD>4.0 (heuristic).
- **verify_trt_fps / benchmark_speed / benchmark_comparison** are single-model transport benchmarks (not end-to-end quality), exit codes only for verify_trt_fps (2 error, 3 NaN/Inf).
- **PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF set at import** of windows_runtime_compat with max_split_size_mb 128 and expandable_segments False (app/windows_runtime_compat.py:21) â€” global side effect, ordering-dependent if torch already imported.
- **Not verified (no execution allowed)**: [INFERENCE] that core.py (outside group) is where HEURISTIC is set and where ORT CUDA EP options (gpu_mem_limit, arena strategy) are composed; those EP option values are NOT in group D files, so CUDA-EP defaults (arena, gpu_mem_limit_gb) cannot be tabulated here other than the axes enumerated in app/roop/benchmark/optimizer.py:635-760 and legacy app/roop/trt_engine.py:81-86.

- No NotImplementedError/TODO/stub in group; "stubword" hits are the word in comments only (index flags).

- process_manager is sequential only and only a library: [INFERENCE] queue path (routes_queue) uses in-process `_run_swap`, not IsolatedVideoBatch, so crash isolation applies only where the caller uses it.
- stage_profiler H2D/D2H transfer bytes are never measured ("unavailable") (stage_profiler.py:~85).

### [E2] ffmpeg/video-IO/persistence
- desktop_capture.py: docstring promises DXGI/ScreenCaptureKit/XComposite/PipeWire; actual = GDI `BitBlt SRCCOPY` only (Win). `_capture_darwin` returns None and Linux enumerate/capture are stubs (app/roop/desktop_capture.py:373-381); `capture_frame` then SILENTLY substitutes a synthetic animated test frame (`_capture_synthetic_fallback`, 428-466) â€“ on mac/Linux or any Win GDI failure the live pipeline "swaps" a fake desktop with no error. Enumeration fallback returns fake windows ("OBS Studio 30.2", hwnd 1001/1002).
- video_io_hw.py: `open_reader`, `open_writer`, `NvZeroCopyReader`, `NvZeroCopyWriter`, `CpuFallbackWriter` have no callers outside the file (grep over app/); only `open_keyframe_reader`/`NvSparseKeyframeReader`/telemetry are consumed (app/roop/face_bank.py:202, app/roop/procmgr_tracking.py:2627). The "zero-copy NVDEC/NVENC" main pass is effectively unused; production decode is the `ffmpeg -hwaccel cuda` pipe (video_stream.py) with host bgr24 copies, encode is the ffmpeg NVENC pipe. QSV decode stated UNVERIFIED on hardware (app/roop/video_io_hw.py:29-31).
- video_io_optimizer.py: whole module (profiler, registry, StreamPreservationSpec) has zero production callers; its `StreamPreservationSpec` uses preset p4/cq14, `-c:a copy` into mp4 (can fail for non-mp4-copyable audio) and `-r {fps:.4f}` (not exact rational).
- Inconsistent encoder defaults: FFMPEG_VideoWriter defaults libx265 crf 14/preset faster (app/roop/ffmpeg_writer.py:171); NVHardwareVideoWriter defaults hevc_nvenc, preset p4, cq 19 (app/roop/video_stream.py:529-533) vs ffmpeg_writer NVENC p5; finalize uses p6/cq19; probe_encoder hard-codes p5 and cq=crf (app/roop/ffmpeg_writer.py:64); video_io_optimizer p4. Comment at app/roop/ffmpeg_path.py:38-46 says these were unified, but video_stream and optimizer still differ. NVHardwareVideoWriter takes `cq=19` and `crf` separatelyâ€”check which wins.
- journal.py: docstring says it verifies `seg_XXXX.mp4` but real segments are `.{base}.segNNNN{ext}`; `JournalManager` (incl. `recover_on_resume`, unsegmented ticks) has no app callers. `segment_writer._write_journal` writes a single `journal.json` into the OUTPUT dir (one file shared by every job rendering into that dir) and `project_checkpoint.update_checkpoint` writes journals with `seg_dir=projects/<id>` where the segments do not live, so `verify_and_recover_segments` on that journal would see missing files and break the chain (inference from app/roop/journal.py:179-230 + app/project_checkpoint.py:395-416).
- journal `probe_segment_with_ffprobe`: a header-only valid file returns (True, 0) and the caller then trusts the manifest's frame count (app/roop/journal.py:171-176,198) â€“ a corrupt-but-parsable tail with wrong frame count is accepted; only the last segment is discarded on failure and earlier bad segments just truncate the chain.
- segment_writer.py: segments always use `FFMPEG_VideoWriter`/NVHardwareVideoWriter per segment â‡’ every segment starts with its own IDR (GOP reset); chunk default 120 s; no per-segment audio. Manifest identity includes `writer_options` containing color plan objects â€“ any change in these invalidates resume (by design). A stale `.resume.json` with changed `hdr` key is discarded (app/roop/segment_writer.py:268-273).
- project_io.py: each `save_project` appends the entire document as a JSONL snapshot to `.roop.journal`, never compacted/truncated â€“ unbounded growth with 120 s autosave on long sessions (app/roop/project_io.py:164-194); `recover_project` takes newest `updated_at >=` file stamp, overwriting the main file.
- project_render.py: `_ensure_checkpoint` returns an existing project checkpoint by `_safe_id` without comparing it to the edited `.roop` document â†’ a re-opened modified `.roop` renders with stale checkpoint settings (app/roop/project_render.py:82-90) [INFERENCE from code path].
- distributed_render.py: no resume across chunks (`chunk workspace is not empty` raises), no retry of failed chunk, single-pass failure raises after all workers finish; GOP-only â‰¥ keyframe granularity so trims must sit on keyframes (hard error); H.264/HEVC sources only; VFR rejected, contradicting vfr_timing support elsewhere; only one worker thread per GPU (no multi-chunk concurrency per card); automation lanes unsupported.
- nle_interchange.py: CMX3600/OTIO/timecode helpers and `catalog_active_face_timecodes` are never called by the app (grep); `face_segments` is only read from `record["timeline"]` (app/roop/project_io.py:103) and app/api.py:913 sets `record["timeline"] = {"scene_cuts": â€¦}` only, so FCPXML/EDL face lanes are empty unless hand-edited. `_timecode` rounds fps with NDF (no 23.976/29.97 drop-frame) and FCPXML format is hard-coded 1920Ã—1080 regardless of the clip; `catalog_active_face_timecodes` defaults to fake range `[(0,100)]` and OTIO defaults `total_frames` to 1800 when unknown (nle_interchange.py:~250,~425).
- util_ffmpeg.py: `join_videos` non-simple branch passes shell-quoted strings as single args (line ~275-285) â€“ broken; `extract_frames`/`create_video` pass both `-vsync cfr` and `-fps_mode cfr` (newer ffmpeg may reject/ warn on both) [INFERENCE]; `cut_video` seeks with `format(start_time, ".2f")` (10 ms precision, not frame-exact, ignores VFR) and no `-avoid_negative_ts`.
- ffmpeg_writer.py: `print(str(cmd))` of the full command on every spawn (line ~417); odd sizes lose one pixel row/col via scale instead of padding; bgr24 rawvideo (8-bit) pipe means 10-bit plans still ingest 8-bit data.
- live_mode.py: every keyframe runs the full `swap_frame` BEFORE `detect_faces` (second detection) â€“ double detection cost on keyframes; tracked frames warp the previous *composited* output (not the raw swap) so errors/blur accumulate across up to 5 tracked frames; `track.output = self.last_output` shared by all faces so multi-face alpha blending applies the same whole-frame warp per face; no temporal landmark smoothing; capture thread no sleep for webcams; desktop capture polling `time.sleep(1/fps)` ignores capture time; frame size mismatch with virtual camera (camera not honoring resolution) raises in `pyvirtualcam.send`.
- virtualcam.py: no enhancer in live mode (`selected_enhancer="None"`), stabilize options all off; RGB fmt assumed available.
- StreamWriter.py: busy-wait spin loop in `LimitFrames` burns a CPU core; `pyvirtualcam.Camera` raises rather than returning None, so the "No virtual camera found!" branch is dead.
- template_parser.py: `{i}` returns int from `data.get("index")`; `str.replace` with an int raises TypeError (app/roop/template_parser.py:5-26) unless caller passes string.
- watchdog.py: detection only (no remediation/abort); `check_tensor` swallows its own errors (returns True); mask-jump check limited to consecutive frames per (stream, thread, shape) key; baseline dict cleared at >512 keys.
- capturer.py: HEVC non-4:2:0 forced to pipe; ffprobe timeouts 30 s, fallback BMP extract 10 s; frame cache keyed on mtime not content; global capture lock. Fallback seek `ts = target / fps` for VFR is inexact (capturer.py:~510).
- finalize_output.py: slack of Â±1 frame when frame count derived from duration; VFR retime errors never fail the render (report only); `stopped` renders skip trim parity; helper-file list covers only `.__audio_cut.mka` and `.resume.json.tmp`.
- post_swap.py: docstring states function bodies were moved byte-for-byte with no test coverage; shared globals (`_progress`, `_make_frame_processor`) injected at import time by api.py â€“ fragile. AI upscale opens `cv2.VideoCapture` on the swapped output (CFR assumption; VFR retime already applied would be lost since it re-encodes via FFMPEG_VideoWriter at `fps*scale`).
- provenance.py: pure re-export; actual watermark/signing in face_engine.pipeline.provenance (not in this group); `enable_provenance_signer` default True, `embed_provenance_watermark` default False.
- keep_awake.py/win_asyncio_compat.py: Windows-only, no-ops elsewhere; keep_awake docstring requires acquire/release on same thread.
- crash_autopsy.py/debug_snapshot.py: diagnostics only; debug_snapshot docstring (4-panel row) vs 2Ã—2 grid code.


### [F] ui/tools/docs/config
- `--help` file committed at app root is a JSON introspection dump, not a CLI help; there is no authoritative flag list here. Actual argparse flags in the codebase (e.g. `--disable-occlusion-mask`, referenced `PHASE2_OCCLUSION.md` Â§4) live in `roop/core.py` (outside group F). Run.py perf env mapping not visible here.
- `default_config.yaml` is not a defaults file: it contains machine-specific `hardware_signature`, `_threads_basis v3|24|10`, `benchmark_results {}`; and contradicts the schema defaults (swap_model hyperswap vs realswap; selected_enhancer `Restore Ultra` vs `UltraMax`; mask_engine `DFL XSeg` vs `RealityUX`; face_detector_size '512' vs 640; use_3d_recon/use_source_bank true vs false; track_stitch, etc.). README says `settings.py` is the real source of defaults ("no config.json fallback") (`README.md:20`).
- Legacy UI cannot represent shipped defaults: dropdown choices lack UltraMax/RealityUX/hyperswap/realswap; swap-model dropdown hard-coded `["inswapper"]` hidden/non-interactive; frontalization widgets hidden+disabled (threshold 30 vs config 15) â†’ UI saves `swap_model`/`frontalization_threshold` into config from invisible controls (`_SESSION_CFG_KEYS` includes `swap_model`, `use_frontalization`, `frontalization_threshold`; `app/ui/main.py:577-587`) and `load_session` can set an enhancer/mask value not in dropdown choices.
- `settings_tab`: `save_config`/`load_config` and `CONFIG_SAVE_DIR` defined but no UI button/dropdown calls them (no wiring in `app/ui/tabs/settings_tab.py:57-69`) â†’ dead code. Max Threads label "default: 3" vs yaml 10 / colab 2. `chk_det_size` default hard-coded True regardless of config. `on_option_changed` calls `raise gr.Error` for unmatched controls.
- `settings_tab.clean_temp`: `shutil.rmtree(os.environ["TEMP"])` with no existence/guard check (`settings_tab.py:~141`).
- `faceswap_tab.start_swap`: `shutil.rmtree(roop.globals.output_path)` if `clear_output` with no `ignore_errors` and no confinement; `gr.Error('No Target Face selected!')` is constructed but never raised (returns instead) (`app/ui/tabs/faceswap_tab.py:2308,2327-2330`).
- `setup_3drecon.py` never fails: `OK = True` is set both initially and in the except branch (line 36 comment "try again implicitly next run"); it claims to check "insightface landmark_3d_68 availability" but never imports/checks insightface; the final banner always prints success (`app/setup_3drecon.py:20-42`).
